# Supplementary Results Master Summary

## 0. Audit status

Audit completed 2026-10-03T16:45:09+07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh). Project root: `/home/vutu/Desktop/Nonlinear_Time_Series_Analysis/Nonlinear-Time-Series-Analysis`. Scope: 926 original files inventoried; 91 notebooks parsed for source and saved outputs, including 479 saved HTML tables; 50 Python scripts structurally read; 528 CSVs fully streamed for schema and row counts; both compressed repeated-session CSVs fully streamed (700000 metric rows plus 100000 matchings). 537 candidate result files (CSV/JSON/GZ in phase1/results, phase1/outputs, phase1/sensitivy_data). Text/code inspection is not a claim of independent recomputation of every experiment. 115 PDF/PNG/SVG files were cataloged; figure content was not visually validated in this audit. Detailed file inventory is in the provenance manifest.
 Audit manifest evidence records labeled VERIFIED CANONICAL: 746 (includes rows of final sensitivity branches, not independent primary results). Five non-Simplex primary effects verified; two Simplex estimands pending canonical choice. Legacy/superseded audit branches identified: 1.

10 issues require human review or conditional recomputation: 1 CRITICAL, 4 HIGH, 4 MEDIUM, 1 LOW. No expensive experiments rerun; original experimental, analysis and manuscript files were not edited. Only stored-result aggregation, exact signed-rank checks, BH checks and hash verification were performed. Missing original inputs prevent an independent raw-data audit.

Status vocabulary in section 17: VERIFIED CANONICAL; LEGACY / SUPERSEDED; CONSISTENT ROUNDING DIFFERENCE; REQUIRES RERUN; UNRESOLVED. “Canonical” for a sensitivity record means the final saved record within that branch, not equivalence to the manuscript nominal estimand. The manifest also uses INVENTORIED for files whose existence/schema was cataloged without numerical canonicalization. Paths below are relative to project root. Notebook cell/output indices are zero-based. Display-only outputs retain their saved precision; NOT STORED means no such inference was found, not a zero or a non-significant result.

## 1. Canonical manuscript anchors

Manuscript: “Ultra-Short PPG Dynamics Across the Wakefulness-to-Drowsiness Transition: A Nonlinear Time-Series Analysis”. Current manuscript values are the audit target, as instructed; they are not silently replaced by the newer frozen Simplex output. Five primary non-Simplex effects and their saved statistics are traceable. Both Simplex manuscript effects are traceable to B, but conflict with the declared A aggregation. The pipeline cannot yet be treated as one internally consistent canonical freeze.

All 20 saved session rows pass integrity/eligibility: strictly increasing timestamps; duplicate timestamps, missing/non-numeric/infinite raw/processed/time entries and invalid labels all zero; labels {0,1}; raw, processed and label rows share the same row axis. The state-support convention assigns each sample the interval to its next sample, caps gaps at the median dt and gives the last sample one median dt. This differs from timestamp-span duration and n_samples/nominal_fs. Support totals: Awake 40902.152528 s; Drowsy 21721.929379 s. Session 01 is 50 Hz; all other rates are close to 25 Hz. Gaps are detected at >1.5 median dt; severe >2 dt, very severe >5 dt. See issue I06 before using rounded hours from the manuscript.

Modern continuous segments split at label changes OR temporal gaps. Older `phase1/notebook/data_audit.ipynb` counts 106 label-only segments (53/state); the 175 modern segments add 69 gap boundaries. The 86 observed state transitions agree. These are different definitions, not two estimates of the same segment count. Saved modern tables contain all 175 segments, 86 transitions and 57 alternating-state patterns (cell8 outputs7/9/11). No original KSS scores, video annotations or timing uncertainty metadata were found: PROVENANCE NOT YET VERIFIED.

P0 1,620,038 samples; T30 1,488,882; T60 1,364,164; S3 1,537,360; S5 1,429,409. Retention fractions are derivable by dividing by P0. The Phase-A potential floor(duration/60) counts (649 Awake/333 Drowsy for P0) are descriptive capacity before the primary segmentation/QC logic, and must not replace the primary included 596/305 counts. Alternative labels also change window origins through gap splitting/re-anchoring; they are not simply a subset of identical P0 windows.

## 4. Quality-control results

Filter: invert PPG, second-order Butterworth bandpass .5–8 Hz, filtfilt (`phase1/src/preprocessing/filter.py`). Artifact screening: complete 5-s blocks with round(fs*5) samples; amplitude Q95−Q05; roughness sqrt(mean(diff(x)^2)); session-wise median/MAD calibration; positive modified z-score >4.5 rejects. Notebook override 4.5 is canonical; the standalone SQI default 3.5 is not the notebook setting. MAD-zero protection uses machine epsilon; a trailing incomplete 5-s block is not screened. Sources: `phase1/src/preprocessing/sqi.py`, `phase1/notebook/data_sqi.ipynb`.

Variance stability: complete 10-s blocks, ddof=0 variance, at least two blocks; S=IQR(v)/max(median(v),epsilon), accept S≤.5 (`phase1/src/segmentation/segmentation.py`; `phase1/notebook/data_segmentation.ipynb`). This is variance stability/quasi-stationarity screening, not proof of full stationarity. Primary segmentation uses same-label runs, non-overlapping round(duration*fs) windows, drops incomplete tails, rejects any flagged sample or within-window gap >1.5/fs; gap rejection does not re-anchor subsequent windows inside the original run. Alternative-label segmentation splits gaps first.

## 6. Phase-space reconstruction

### 6.1 AMI

Implementation: `phase1/src/embedding/ami.py`, `phase1/notebook/ami.ipynb`. KSG estimator1, k=3, joint Chebyshev cKDTree distances, digamma terms, natural-log information units; strict marginal neighborhoods achieved with nextafter(epsilon,0). No amplitude histogram bins. Candidate delays 1…round(fs*1.0s); first strict interior local minimum, no endpoint fallback. JITTER=0; RANDOM_STATE=42 is inactive when jitter is zero. Computed independently by window/state/representation. Primary processed 60-s 901/901 windows have a selected tau. The consensus .16 s is the median of 20 session medians, not the window mode (.12 s). Fourteen of20 session medians lie within .16±.04 s. This is an operational common delay, not a state-specific optimal embedding claim.

### 6.2 FNN

Implementation: `phase1/src/embedding/fnn.py`, `phase1/notebook/fnn.ipynb`. Dimensions1…10; Kennel-style false neighbor if relative additional-coordinate distance/R_m>15 OR full m+1 distance/std(signal)>2. Euclidean nearest neighbor, no temporal exclusion (Theiler0) in this diagnostic; zero/near-zero distances excluded; cKDTree candidate search expands if required. First dimension with FNN≤1% is a diagnostic selected_m; fixed m8 does not guarantee ≤1% for every session. Curves/valid counts are saved in `phase1/results/fnn/fnn_curves.csv`; session/state curves in outputs/phase_space/tables. Rationale: low FNN plateau and a uniform dimension for comparisons, not a unique elbow. FNN can increase again at m9.

## 7. Simplex Projection

### 7.1 Frozen implementation

Sources: `phase1/src/prediction/simplex_projection.py`, `phase1/notebook/simplex_projection.ipynb`, `phase1/sensitivy_data/primary_simplex_windowfirst_v1/manifest.json`, `phase1/sensitivy_data/freeze_primary_simplex_windowfirst_v1.py`. Processed waveform is z-scored within each window using whole-window mean and population std(ddof0). Forward embedding, Euclidean neighbors k=m+1=9, exact batched cKDTree query with enough candidates for exclusions. Leave-one-out includes self exclusion at W0; admissibility is |j−i|>round(fs*1.0). Both query and neighbor target at horizon h must lie within the same window; valid state indices run from (m−1)tau through N−h−1, and library changes by horizon. Zero-distance neighbors (≤epsilon) share weights; otherwise exp(−d_j/d_1) normalized by sum. Correlation is Pearson CC; NRMSE denominator is the whole-window population std (1 after z-score), not the horizon target subset std. No cross-window library. Primary full horizon freeze has 16,218 unique rows, 901 windows ×18; all horizon counts18, no missing horizon rows and support minimum1.0. Primary h offsets are distinct after rounding at the actual recorded fs values.

### 7.4 Canonicalization audit

Let y_wh be a horizon-specific metric. A = median_w[(1/18)Σ_h y_wh] (declared method; frozen window-first v1). B = (1/18)Σ_h median_w[y_wh] (current manuscript numerical anchors; original main prediction notebook). Both then form session Δ=Drowsy−Awake and the overall median of20 session Δ. Median and mean do not commute. The underlying 901 windows, processed labels, 18 horizons, sample-rate conversion and normalization agree. Independent reconstruction of the720 horizon-median cells matches old CC within5.55e−16 and NRMSE within1.11e−16; frozen901 window means agree within1.11e−16 and session medians exactly. The difference is aggregation order, not a different horizon definition or cohort.

## 8. RQA

### 8.1 Frozen implementation

Sources: `phase1/src/rqa/rqa.py`, `phase1/notebook/rqa.ipynb`, `phase1/verification_result/verification_ntsa_parameter/verification_rqa.ipynb`. Euclidean forward embedding. Eligible upper triangle has |i−j|>W; W=(m−1)tau_samples=28 at25Hz and56 at50Hz (physical1.12s). LOI and excluded diagonals removed. Historical nominal cache uses per-window linear quantile for epsilon at RR=.02, recurrence distance≤epsilon. Current tie-safe selection uses ceil(target RR×eligible_count), evaluates the upper order statistic and strict lower threshold, chooses closest achieved RR, ties choose lower; epsilon tolerance4 machine eps. It preserves historical epsilon only if the recurrence set is the selected one. Zero-distance fraction≥RR can yield epsilon0 and is checked for degeneracy. DET uses diagonal runs length≥2 in the eligible upper triangle, normalized by upper-triangle recurrence count. Vertical runs use the full symmetric matrix, length≥2. Lmean and TT are sample-index line lengths, not seconds; sample-rate mixing should be remembered when interpreting their values.

Current nominal cache differs from original in55 session12 windows (TT17 of those): epsilon max absolute difference .04923333843078; achieved RR max9.61166e−7; DET6.939362384028591e−5; LAM8.888236131074367e−5; TT3.870879407008587e−5. The mismatch is explained by historical quantile interpolation, not embedding/masks. Nominal session effects agree at saved display precision. Audit issue I09 retains the window-level algorithm distinction.

## 9. Rosenstein LLE

### 9.1 Frozen implementation

Sources: `phase1/src/chaos/lyapunov.py`, `phase1/notebook/lyapunov.ipynb`, `phase1/verification_result/verification_ntsa_parameter/verification_lle.ipynb`. Spectral mean period: demean waveform, one-sided rfft squared magnitude, exclude DC and include all positive frequencies through Nyquist (no additional .5–8Hz mask); f_mean=ΣfP/ΣP, T=1/f_mean. Theiler=max(1,round(fs*T)); admissible neighbor |j−i|>Theiler. First nearest admissible Euclidean neighbor is selected; then distances≤10epsilon×max(std(states),1)×sqrt(m) are rejected without replacing by next positive-distance neighbor. Evolve to floor(5fs) samples, discard out-of-endpoint pairs separately at each lag, average ln Euclidean distances. linregress(mean log distance versus seconds); R2=rvalue². Fit inclusive .80–1.30s, ≥3 finite fit points each with≥30 pairs; ≥50 initial pairs; finite slope and R2≥.90. LLE units s^-1. Meanlog is unscaled and time axis is seconds; manuscript equations using fs-scaled values versus sample indices are equivalent only if the axis/scale convention is paired correctly. This finite-window estimate is not proof of deterministic chaos.

Fullprecision caches `phase1/results/lle/verification/fit_range_bc330dd9c3e1.csv` and `theiler_bc330dd9c3e1.csv`,2703 rows each.

## 10. PPS surrogate testing

### 10.1 Generation

Implementation `phase1/src/surrogates/pps.py`; calibration `phase1/notebook/pps.ipynb`; metrics `phase1/src/surrogates/statistic_test.py`, `phase1/notebook/statistic.ipynb`; LLE `phase1/notebook/statistic_test_lle.ipynb`. Forward embedding m8/tau .16s on processed PPG. Select initial embedded index uniformly from all embedded states, including last. Candidate transitions j range0…Ne−2 (last has no successor). Probability proportional to exp(−(distance_j−min_distance)/rho); after categorical draw j, move to j+1. Self/temporal candidates allowed; no boundary wrap. Emit first coordinate, same original signal length N. Surrogate waveforms themselves are not frozen on disk; seeds and metric distributions are saved.

rho calibration optimizes the number C2 of maximal consecutive-index runs with length≥2 (not exactly length2). Three independent trials per grid point,21 geometric grid points, first argmax on ties. Master seed20260825; identity hash first4 SHA256 bytes little-endian; trial uint32 seeds from the window RNG. Current notebook grids: Processed5…600, Raw5…1000, output pps_radius_calibration_update (absent). Earlier report says5…200/5…400. Saved901 nominal processed radii include mixed old/new compatible grids, see I03. Actual rho used matches calibration CSV within2.842170943040401e−14.

M39 verified for each of901 windows for six metrics:35,139 ensemble rows. Six-metric master seed2026; stable sorted compact-JSON complete-window identity hashed to first8 little-endian SHA256 bytes; surrogate ID0…38 seeds derived from window seed and ID. PPS six-metric computation receives processed amplitude without explicit z-scoring; Simplex NRMSE uses whole-window std and its original metrics match frozen z-scored A within2.22e−16. LLE uses a separate ensemble: master20260828, IDs1…39, saved901×39 outputs;872 original R2-qualified windows contribute34,008 surrogates to RQ1. Original LLE Theiler from original waveform is reused for surrogates; surrogate R2 minimum0.0, so R2 is diagnostic there, not the original .90 filter. Do not imply all seven metrics used identical39 generated waveforms.

### 10.2 Rank test

Actual stored two-sided test is tie-inclusive: p=min(1, 2×min(1+#{surrogate≤original}, 1+#{surrogate≥original})/(M+1)). Stored ascending midrank r=1+#{surrogate<original}+.5#{equal}. Requested p=min(1,2min(r,M+2−r)/(M+1)) agrees only without ties. Audit finds24 exactly tied surrogate comparisons in six window-metric tests; their midrank p differs from stored tie-inclusive p, but none changes rejection at .05. No ties in included LLE. All5406 six-metric p-values and872 included LLE p-values independently reproduce with zero mismatches. M39 gives p_min=.05; rejection uses p≤.05, not p<.05. Window rank p values are unadjusted; session-gap inference has a separate BH family of12 six-metric×state tests.

### 10.4 PPS diagnostics

Saved distributions: `phase1/results/statistic/processed/*_pps_surrogate_results.csv`, window summaries `*_pps_window_results.csv`; separate LLE `phase1/results/lle/surrogates/*_lle_pps_surrogates.csv`. Integrity checks are saved in `phase1/outputs/statistic/rq1_pps_integrity_report.csv`. Rank summary anchors CC Awake median .6066017316017316 (~60.7%), Drowsy .7100840336134454 (~71.0%); DET medians1 in both states. This is not the pooled62.75%/75.08% CC rejection percentage. Rho calibration is not a sensitivity analysis of final rejection against rho; no final rho-perturbation RQ1 test was found.

## 13. Label sensitivity

P0 current manuscript B anchors are shown in section11. T30/T60/S3/S5 computations use window-first A. Thus label-sensitivity direction anchors are verified, but the P0 numerical baseline in the displayed manuscript is not the same aggregation. All rules exceptS5 have20 paired sessions; S5 has19 because session15 lacks valid Drowsy windows. Full per-window all7 metrics exist in `phase1/results/ntsa_label_sensitive/{rule}/session_*_ntsa.csv`,80 files. LLE inclusion uses LLE_pass_R2_090. ## 14. Unknown repeated-session dependence

### 14.1 Random hypothetical pairings

Final saved experiment: `phase1/sensitivy_data/unknown_repeated_session_dependence_v1/run_analysis.py`, protocol.json, manifest.json. Its20-session seven-metric input uses hash-verified frozen A Simplex, plus primary median-session RQA/LLE. Independent comparison gives zero Simplex differences; RQA/LLE paired inputs retain source CSV rounding. It is not an older superseded analysis, but it is NOT the manuscript B Simplex pipeline. Conditional on selecting A, these results are internally consistent and do not need rerun; conditional on retaining B, pairing and adversarial summaries must be recalculated with B inputs (inexpensive session-level operation).

100,000 random perfect matchings from NumPy default_rng20260930: permute20 sessions, group adjacent indices into10 pairs, sort pairs/canonicalize; sampling with replacement. Theoretical20!/(2^10×10!)=654,729,075. Saved outputs contain99,990 unique matchings and10 duplicates (fraction .0001=.01%). Cluster Δ is arithmetic mean of the two session Δ values; effect is median of10 cluster Δ. Exact conditional signed-rank enumeration discards |Δ|≤1e−12, average ranks for equal absolute differences; p two-sided, r_rb=(W+−W−)/(W++W−). All-zero gives p1 and undefined r. BH over all7 metrics separately in each matching; never over100,000 matchings. No nested bootstrap. Recomputed BH agrees for all700,000 metric rows, maximum error0.

Checkpoints1k/10k/50k/100k are prefixes of one RNG stream. The50k→100k headline maximum effect-median shift is about2.273e−5; maximum effect-tail shift about9.067e−5; maximum q-frequency shift .116 percentage points. This supports Monte Carlo stability for the saved A experiment, not validity of the hypothetical participant assignment.

### 14.3 Conservative/adversarial search

Four headline metrics ×four objectives ×100 starts=1600 local searches; starts are the100 most conservative sampled pairings per objective, not100 newly independent random starts. Objectives minimize expected-sign×median, expected-sign×r_rb, expected-direction cluster count, or maximize raw p. At each step evaluate90 neighbor matchings:45 choices of pair-pairs ×2 cross-rearrangements. Steepest improvement >1e−12; lexicographic tie break. BH7 is evaluated from all metric results for the candidate pairing. No global optimum is proven; report “best found”, not worst possible. Complete start logs are saved in adversarial_local_search_starts_v1.csv.

### 14.4 Interpretation limits

Ten two-session clusters are hypothetical and depend on the unverified10-participant/two-session design claim. Unknown participant-session linkage is not recovered by matching. The cluster mean changes the estimand from session median differences, and the seven-metric BH threshold depends on the whole family. These analyses assess dependence sensitivity; they do not repair a missing identifier or create participant-level primary inference. Sensitivity draws are not independent replications. Sign preservation is distinct from q support.

## 15. Symbolic dynamics

`phase1/src/symbolic/symbolic.py`, `phase1/notebook/symbolic.ipynb`, `phase1/main/symbolic_main.ipynb`. Symbols are encoded from detected PPG peak-to-peak intervals (PPI), not raw amplitude or ordinal patterns. find_peaks on processed waveform without extra smoothing, minimum distance ceil(.30fs), prominence .2×(P95−P5). PPI=index_difference/fs retained in [.30,2.0]s; interval coverage≥90%; count≥max(3,ceil(window_duration/2)−2). Abrupt relative PPI change>.2 is a diagnostic, not a rejection rule. Six equal-width bins per window between min/max PPI; internal boundaries use searchsorted(...,side=right), constant series symbol0. Overlapping triples:0V both adjacent differences0;1V exactly one0;2LV both nonzero same sign;2UV both nonzero opposite signs;2V=2LV+2UV. These match the six-level variation-category mathematical definitions associated with Porta/Guzzetti, but on PPG-derived intervals without evidence of ECG RR validation. No clinical autonomic equivalence is established. Per-window0V+1V+2V=100%; separately aggregated session medians need not sum100%.

## 16. Statistical conventions and multiplicity

Computational unit: window; primary inferential unit: recording session. RQA/LLE state aggregation is median valid windows; Simplex methodA is mean18 horizons per window then median windows, but manuscript numbers useB. Paired Δ=Drowsy−Awake; statistic median of paired sessionΔ. Ordinary20,000 resamples of paired Δ vector with replacement, median per resample, percentile2.5/97.5 CI. No BCa/studentization, no participant clustering in primary bootstrap. Rank effect r_rb=(W+−W−)/(W++W−) after dropping zero differences; no tie denominator shortcut. Under some saved SciPy method=auto signed-rank calculations, ties/zero handling can lead to approximation; do not call every branch exact solely by intent.

BH implementation sorts raw p, computes m*p/rank, applies reverse cumulative minimum and clips at1; supports q<.05 in primary/repeated branch. PPS window ranks reject p≤.05 because attainable minimum=.05. Some evidence flags use≤ instead of< at the threshold; no primary stored q equals.05. Domain prediction CSV advertises an old6-test duration family, but canonical primary is7 once; old RQA inferential summary corrects42 tests and uses mean-session estimand; neither supplies current primary q. CI and Wilcoxon p need not agree because percentile median CI and signed-rank test target different distributional properties.

No unresolved point authorizes overwriting current results.
